00001         * PACMAIN : DRIVER ONLY. NO GAME RULES, NO I/O STATEMENTS.
00002         * PACPLAT (HOST)  PACINPT (CARDS)  PACGAME (RULES)  PACMAP (MAP)
00003         * PACAI (GHOSTS)  PACDISP (TEXT OUTPUT). SEE DOCS/ARCHITECTURE.MD
00004         * PARM 'TSO' [AIL N] REQUESTS THE INTERACTIVE TSO ADAPTER
00005         * (MVS-8): CONFIG COMES FROM THE PARM, THE BOARD IS SHOWN BEFORE
00006         * THE FIRST PROMPT. NO PARM = BATCH CARD MODE (REFERENCE).
00007         * NO INPUT IS READ ONCE THE GAME HAS ENDED (QUIT, WON, LOST).
00008         * OPTIONAL 'M' IN PARM COLUMN 11 (E.G. 'BAT       M' OR
00009         * 'TSO AI3 2 M') MAKES PACDISP PRINT A FINAL METRICS LINE.
00010         * COLUMN 12 = COLOUR (MVS-G1): A AUTO, Y YES, N/BLANK NO. PARM
00011         * 'TSD ...' IS THE BATCH DRY RUN OF THE COLOUR SCREENS (HEX).
00012         * COLUMN 13 = HIGHLIGHT (MVS-G2): A AUTO, Y YES, N/BLANK NO.
00013          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00014          PROGRAM-ID. PACMAIN.
00015          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00016          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00017          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00018          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00019          DATA DIVISION.
00020          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00021          01  PAC-STATE COPY PACSTATE.
00022 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00023 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00024 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00025 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00026 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00027 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.
00028 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00029 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00030 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00031 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00032 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00033 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.
00034 C            05  GAME-STATE            PIC X(7).
00035 C            05  LAST-CMD              PIC X.
00036 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00037 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00038 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00039 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00040 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00041 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00042 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00043 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00044 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00045 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00046 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00047 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00048 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00049          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00050 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00051 C        01  PAC-REQ.

00054 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.   2


00055 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00056 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00057 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00058 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00059 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00060 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00061 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00062 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00063 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00064 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00065 C            05  REQ-HILITE            PIC X.
00066          01  WS-CFG.
00067              05  WS-CFG-CH         PIC X OCCURS 5 TIMES.
00068          01  WS-K                  PIC 99.
00069          LINKAGE SECTION.
00070          01  PARM-AREA.
00071              05  PARM-LEN          PIC S9(4) COMP.
00072              05  PARM-MODE         PIC X(3).
00073              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00074              05  PARM-CFG          PIC X(5).
00075              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00076              05  PARM-MET          PIC X.
00077              05  PARM-COLOR        PIC X.
00078              05  PARM-HILITE       PIC X.
00079          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PARM-AREA.
00080          MAIN-PARA.
00081              MOVE 'BAT' TO REQ-MODE.
00082              MOVE SPACE TO REQ-METRIC.
00083              IF PARM-LEN > 10 AND PARM-MET = 'M'
00084                  MOVE 'M' TO REQ-METRIC.
00085              IF PARM-LEN > 2 AND PARM-MODE = 'TSO'
00086                  MOVE 'TSO' TO REQ-MODE.
00087              IF PARM-LEN > 2 AND PARM-MODE = 'TSD'
00088                  MOVE 'TSD' TO REQ-MODE.
00089              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-COLOR.
00090              IF PARM-LEN > 11
00091                  MOVE PARM-COLOR TO REQ-COLOR.
00092              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00093              IF PARM-LEN > 12
00094                  MOVE PARM-HILITE TO REQ-HILITE.
00095              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00096              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00097              MOVE 'RSET' TO REQ-OP.
00098              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00099              MOVE 'OPEN' TO REQ-OP.
00100              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00101              MOVE 'HEAD' TO REQ-OP.
00102              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00103              IF REQ-MODE = 'BAT'
00104                  PERFORM BAT-START THRU BAT-START-EXIT
00105              ELSE
00106                  PERFORM TSO-START THRU TSO-START-EXIT.
00107              PERFORM TURN-PARA THRU TURN-PARA-EXIT
00108                  UNTIL F-EOF = 1 OR F-QUIT = 1 OR F-WON = 1
00109                     OR F-LOST = 1.
00110              MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP.
00111              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.   3


00112              MOVE 'CLOS' TO REQ-OP.
00113              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00114              MOVE 'FINI' TO REQ-OP.
00115              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00116              STOP RUN.
00117          BAT-START.
00118              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00119              PERFORM CHECK-CONFIG THRU CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.
00120              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00121          BAT-START-EXIT.
00122              EXIT.
00123          TSO-START.
00124              IF PARM-LEN > 4
00125                  PERFORM COPY-PARM-CFG
00126                  MOVE 'PARM' TO REQ-OP
00127                  CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ
00128                  PERFORM APPLY-CONFIG THRU APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00129              PERFORM SHOW-START.
00130              PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00131          TSO-START-EXIT.
00132              EXIT.
00133          COPY-PARM-CFG.
00134         * ONLY PARM-LEN BYTES ARE VALID: BLANK WHAT LIES BEYOND THEM.
00135              MOVE PARM-CFG TO WS-CFG.
00136              COMPUTE WS-K = PARM-LEN - 3.
00137              PERFORM BLANK-CFG-CH UNTIL WS-K > 5.
00138              MOVE WS-CFG TO REQ-TEXT.
00139          BLANK-CFG-CH.
00140              MOVE SPACE TO WS-CFG-CH (WS-K).
00141              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00142          SHOW-START.
00143              MOVE 'INIT' TO REQ-OP.
00144              CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00145              MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP.
00146              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00147          APPLY-CONFIG.
00148              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00149                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00150                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00151          APPLY-CONFIG-EXIT.
00152              EXIT.
00153          READ-INPUT.
00154              MOVE 'PRMT' TO REQ-OP.
00155              CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00156              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00157              CALL 'PACINPT' USING PAC-REQ.
00158              IF REQ-KIND = 'EOF'
00159                  MOVE 'EOF ' TO REQ-OP
00160                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00161          READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00162              EXIT.
00163          CHECK-CONFIG.
00164              IF REQ-KIND = 'CFG'
00165                  MOVE 'CFG ' TO REQ-OP
00166                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00167                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00168          CHECK-CONFIG-EXIT.   4


00169              EXIT.
00170          TURN-PARA.
00171              PERFORM DISPATCH THRU DISPATCH-EXIT.
00172              IF F-QUIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND F-LOST = 0
00173                  PERFORM READ-INPUT THRU READ-INPUT-EXIT.
00174          TURN-PARA-EXIT.
00175              EXIT.
00176          DISPATCH.
00177              IF REQ-KIND = 'MOV'
00178                  MOVE 'MOVE' TO REQ-OP
00179                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00180                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00181                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00182              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'QUI'
00183                  MOVE 'QUIT' TO REQ-OP
00184                  CALL 'PACGAME' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00185              ELSE IF REQ-KIND = 'RDR'
00186                  MOVE 'STAT' TO REQ-OP
00187                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ
00188              ELSE
00189                  MOVE 'INVL' TO REQ-OP
00190                  CALL 'PACDISP' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00191          DISPATCH-EXIT.
00192              EXIT.   5

00001         * PACGAME : GAME RULES AND STATE (PORTABLE, NO TERMINAL I/O).
00002         * OPS : RSET  DEFAULTS (3 LIVES, PLAYER 2,2, AI1, 1 GHOST)
00003         *       CFG   APPLY REQ-LEVEL / REQ-COUNT FROM THE CONFIG CARD
00004         *       INIT  LOAD MAP, PLACE GHOSTS, COUNT PELLETS
00005         *       MOVE  ONE TURN (REQ-DIR, REQ-CHAR)   QUIT  EOF

00065 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00066 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.

00078          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00079          MAIN-PARA.
00080              IF REQ-OP = 'RSET'
00081                  PERFORM DO-RESET
00082              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CFG '
00083                  PERFORM DO-CONFIG
00084              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00085                  PERFORM DO-INIT
00086              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'MOVE'
00087                  PERFORM DO-MOVE
00088              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'QUIT'
00089                  PERFORM DO-QUIT
00090              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'EOF '
00091                  MOVE 1 TO F-EOF.
00092              GOBACK.
00093          DO-RESET.
00094              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X.
00095              MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y.
00096              MOVE 0 TO TICK-COUNT.
00097              MOVE 0 TO SCORE.
00098              MOVE 0 TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00099              MOVE 3 TO LIVES.
00100              MOVE 'READY  ' TO GAME-STATE.
00101              MOVE ' ' TO LAST-CMD.
00102              MOVE 'START  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00103              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00104              MOVE 0 TO F-LOST.
00105              MOVE 0 TO F-QUIT.
00106              MOVE 0 TO F-EOF.
00107              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00108              MOVE 1 TO AI-LEVEL.
00109              MOVE 1 TO GHOST-COUNT.
00110              MOVE 12345 TO GHOST-SEED.
00111          DO-CONFIG.   3


00112              MOVE REQ-LEVEL TO AI-LEVEL.
00113              MOVE REQ-COUNT TO GHOST-COUNT.
00114          DO-INIT.
00115              MOVE 'LOAD' TO M-OP.
00116              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00117              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00118              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00119              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00120              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00121              PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00122                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00123              PERFORM CLEAR-GHOST-CELL VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00124                  UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00125              MOVE 'CNT ' TO M-OP.
00126              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00127              MOVE M-COUNT TO PELLETS-LEFT.
00128          RESET-GHOST.
00129              MOVE GS-X (WS-G) TO GH-X (WS-G).
00130              MOVE GS-Y (WS-G) TO GH-Y (WS-G).
00131              MOVE GS-DIR (WS-G) TO GH-DIR (WS-G).
00132          CLEAR-GHOST-CELL.
00133              MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO M-X.
00134              MOVE GH-Y (WS-G) TO M-Y.
00135              MOVE 'CLR ' TO M-OP.
00136              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00137          DO-QUIT.
00138              MOVE 1 TO F-QUIT.
00139              MOVE 'QUIT   ' TO GAME-STATE.
00140          DO-MOVE.
00141              MOVE REQ-CHAR TO LAST-CMD.
00142              MOVE PLAYER-X TO NEW-X.
00143              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO NEW-Y.
00144              MOVE 'MOVED  ' TO LAST-RESULT.
00145              PERFORM AIM-MOVE THRU AIM-MOVE-EXIT.
00146              ADD 1 TO TICK-COUNT.
00147              MOVE NEW-X TO M-X.
00148              MOVE NEW-Y TO M-Y.
00149              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00150              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00151              IF M-FLAG = 1
00152                  MOVE 'BLOCKED' TO LAST-RESULT
00153              ELSE
00154                  MOVE NEW-X TO PLAYER-X
00155                  MOVE NEW-Y TO PLAYER-Y
00156                  PERFORM EAT-PELLET THRU EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00157              PERFORM ROUND-END THRU ROUND-END-EXIT.
00158              PERFORM SET-STATE THRU SET-STATE-EXIT.
00159          AIM-MOVE.
00160              IF REQ-DIR = 'U'
00161                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-Y
00162              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'D'
00163                  ADD 1 TO NEW-Y
00164              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'L'
00165                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM NEW-X
00166              ELSE IF REQ-DIR = 'R'
00167                  ADD 1 TO NEW-X.
00168          AIM-MOVE-EXIT.   4


00169              EXIT.
00170          EAT-PELLET.
00171              MOVE PLAYER-X TO M-X.
00172              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO M-Y.
00173              MOVE 'EAT ' TO M-OP.
00174              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00175              IF M-FLAG = 1
00176                  ADD 10 TO SCORE
00177                  SUBTRACT 1 FROM PELLETS-LEFT.
00178              IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00179                  MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00180          EAT-PELLET-EXIT.
00181              EXIT.
00182          ROUND-END.
00183              MOVE 0 TO F-HIT.
00184              PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00185              IF F-HIT = 0 AND F-WON = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 0
00186                  CALL 'PACAI' USING PAC-STATE
00187                  PERFORM CHECK-HIT THRU CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00188              IF F-HIT = 1
00189                  PERFORM LOSE-LIFE THRU LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00190          ROUND-END-EXIT.
00191              EXIT.
00192          CHECK-HIT.
00193              PERFORM HIT-TEST THRU HIT-TEST-EXIT
00194                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00195          CHECK-HIT-EXIT.
00196              EXIT.
00197          HIT-TEST.
00198              IF GH-X (WS-G) = PLAYER-X AND GH-Y (WS-G) = PLAYER-Y
00199                  MOVE 1 TO F-HIT.
00200          HIT-TEST-EXIT.
00201              EXIT.
00202          LOSE-LIFE.
00203              MOVE 0 TO F-WON.
00204              SUBTRACT 1 FROM LIVES.
00205              IF LIVES = 0
00206                  MOVE 1 TO F-LOST
00207                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00208              ELSE
00209                  MOVE 'DIED   ' TO LAST-RESULT
00210                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-X
00211                  MOVE 2 TO PLAYER-Y
00212                  PERFORM RESET-GHOST VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1
00213                      UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT
00214                  IF PELLETS-LEFT = 0
00215                      MOVE 1 TO F-WON.
00216          LOSE-LIFE-EXIT.
00217              EXIT.
00218          SET-STATE.
00219              IF F-LOST = 1
00220                  MOVE 'LOST   ' TO GAME-STATE
00221              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00222                  MOVE 'WON    ' TO GAME-STATE
00223              ELSE
00224                  MOVE 'RUNNING' TO GAME-STATE.
00225          SET-STATE-EXIT.   5


00226              EXIT.   6

00059 C        01  MAP-REQ.

00065          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00066          MAIN-PARA.
00067              MOVE 0 TO M-FLAG.
00068              IF M-OP = 'LOAD'
00069                  MOVE MAP-DATA TO MAP-AREA
00070              ELSE IF M-OP = 'WALL'
00071                  PERFORM DO-WALL THRU DO-WALL-EXIT
00072              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CLR '
00073                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00074              ELSE IF M-OP = 'EAT '
00075                  PERFORM DO-EAT THRU DO-EAT-EXIT
00076              ELSE IF M-OP = 'CNT '
00077                  PERFORM DO-COUNT.
00078              GOBACK.
00079          DO-WALL.
00080              IF M-X < 1 OR M-X > 20 OR M-Y < 1 OR M-Y > 10
00081                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG
00082              ELSE IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '#'
00083                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00084          DO-WALL-EXIT.
00085              EXIT.
00086          DO-EAT.
00087              IF MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X) = '.'
00088                  MOVE ' ' TO MAP-CELL (M-Y M-X)
00089                  MOVE 1 TO M-FLAG.
00090          DO-EAT-EXIT.
00091              EXIT.
00092          DO-COUNT.
00093              MOVE 0 TO M-COUNT.
00094              PERFORM COUNT-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00095                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.
00096          COUNT-ROW.
00097              PERFORM COUNT-CELL THRU COUNT-CELL-EXIT
00098                  VARYING WS-COL FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-COL > 20.
00099          COUNT-CELL.
00100              IF MAP-CELL (WS-ROW WS-COL) = '.'
00101                  ADD 1 TO M-COUNT.
00102          COUNT-CELL-EXIT.
00103              EXIT.   3

00001         * PACAI : GHOST MOVEMENT FOR ALL ACTIVE GHOSTS (PORTABLE).
00002         * AI-LEVEL 1 RANDOM, 2 PERSISTENT DIRECTION, 3 CHASE (MANHATTAN).
00003         * LCG SEED IN THE STATE (FIXED 12345) KEEPS RUNS REPRODUCIBLE.
00004         * WALL TESTS GO THROUGH PACMAP. NO NESTED SUBSCRIPTS (D13).
00005          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00006          PROGRAM-ID. PACAI.
00007          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00008          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00009          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00010          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00011          DATA DIVISION.
00012          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00013          01  MAP-REQ COPY PACMREQ.
00014 C       * MAP SERVICE REQUEST: M-OP = LOAD WALL CLR  EAT  CNT
00015 C        01  MAP-REQ.
00016 C            05  M-OP                  PIC X(4).
00017 C            05  M-X                   PIC 99.
00018 C            05  M-Y                   PIC 99.

00022          01  WS-QUOT               PIC 9(9).
00023          01  WS-REM                PIC 9(5).
00024          01  WS-D                  PIC 9.
00025          01  WS-G                  PIC 9.
00026          01  WS-J                  PIC 9.
00027          01  WS-K                  PIC 9.
00028          01  WS-CUR                PIC 9.
00029          01  WS-REVD               PIC 9.
00030          01  CAND-N                PIC 9.

00033          01  DIR-DATA              PIC X(8) VALUE '10120121'.
00034          01  DIR-TABLE REDEFINES DIR-DATA.
00035              05  DIR-ENTRY         OCCURS 4 TIMES.
00036                  10  DIR-DX        PIC 9.
00037                  10  DIR-DY        PIC 9.

00039          01  REV-TABLE REDEFINES REV-DATA.
00040              05  REV-D             PIC 9 OCCURS 4 TIMES.

00042          01  G-NY                  PIC 99.
00043          01  DIST-X                PIC S99.
00044          01  DIST-Y                PIC S99.
00045          01  DIST-T                PIC S999.
00046          01  BEST-DIST             PIC S999.
00047          LINKAGE SECTION.
00048          01  PAC-STATE COPY PACSTATE.
00049 C       * SHARED GAME STATE. NO VALUE CLAUSES (USED IN LINKAGE TOO).
00050 C       * ONLY PACGAME/PACMAP/PACAI MODIFY IT; PACDISP READS IT.
00051 C        01  PAC-STATE.
00052 C            05  MAP-AREA.
00053 C                10  MAP-ROW           OCCURS 10 TIMES.
00054 C                    15  MAP-CELL      PIC X OCCURS 20 TIMES.   2


00055 C            05  PLAYER-X              PIC 99.
00056 C            05  PLAYER-Y              PIC 99.
00057 C            05  TICK-COUNT            PIC 999.
00058 C            05  SCORE                 PIC 9(4).
00059 C            05  PELLETS-LEFT          PIC 999.
00060 C            05  LIVES                 PIC 9.

00063 C            05  LAST-RESULT           PIC X(7).
00064 C            05  F-WON                 PIC 9.
00065 C            05  F-LOST                PIC 9.
00066 C            05  F-QUIT                PIC 9.
00067 C            05  F-EOF                 PIC 9.
00068 C            05  F-HIT                 PIC 9.
00069 C            05  AI-LEVEL              PIC 9.
00070 C            05  GHOST-COUNT           PIC 9.
00071 C            05  GHOST-SEED            PIC 9(5).
00072 C            05  GH-ENTRY              OCCURS 3 TIMES.
00073 C                10  GH-X              PIC 99.
00074 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00075 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00076          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE.
00077          MAIN-PARA.
00078              PERFORM GHOST-MOVE THRU GHOST-MOVE-EXIT
00079                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00080              GOBACK.
00081          GHOST-MOVE.
00082              MOVE 0 TO CAND-N.
00083              MOVE GH-DIR (WS-G) TO WS-CUR.
00084              MOVE REV-D (WS-CUR) TO WS-REVD.
00085              PERFORM GHOST-PROBE THRU GHOST-PROBE-EXIT
00086                  VARYING WS-D FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-D > 4.
00087              IF CAND-N = 0 AND AI-LEVEL > 1
00088                  PERFORM TRY-REVERSE THRU TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00089              IF CAND-N > 0
00090                  PERFORM CHOOSE-DIR THRU CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT
00091                  PERFORM STEP-GHOST.
00092          GHOST-MOVE-EXIT.
00093              EXIT.
00094          GHOST-PROBE.
00095              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00096              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00097              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.
00098              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00099              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00100              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00101              IF M-FLAG = 0
00102                  IF AI-LEVEL = 1 OR WS-D NOT = WS-REVD
00103                      ADD 1 TO CAND-N
00104                      MOVE WS-D TO CAND (CAND-N).
00105          GHOST-PROBE-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.
00107          TRY-REVERSE.
00108              MOVE WS-REVD TO WS-D.
00109              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00110              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00111              MOVE G-NX TO M-X.   3


00112              MOVE G-NY TO M-Y.
00113              MOVE 'WALL' TO M-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACMAP' USING PAC-STATE MAP-REQ.
00115              IF M-FLAG = 0
00116                  MOVE 1 TO CAND-N
00117                  MOVE WS-D TO CAND (1).
00118          TRY-REVERSE-EXIT.
00119              EXIT.
00120          CHOOSE-DIR.
00121              IF AI-LEVEL = 3
00122                  PERFORM PICK-CHASE
00123              ELSE IF AI-LEVEL = 2 AND CAND-N = 1
00124                  MOVE CAND (1) TO WS-D
00125              ELSE
00126                  PERFORM PICK-RANDOM.
00127          CHOOSE-DIR-EXIT.
00128              EXIT.
00129          PICK-RANDOM.
00130              COMPUTE WS-WORK = GHOST-SEED * 75 + 74.
00131              DIVIDE WS-WORK BY 65537 GIVING WS-QUOT
00132                  REMAINDER GHOST-SEED.
00133              DIVIDE GHOST-SEED BY CAND-N GIVING WS-QUOT
00134                  REMAINDER WS-REM.
00135              ADD 1 TO WS-REM.
00136              MOVE CAND (WS-REM) TO WS-D.
00137          PICK-CHASE.
00138              MOVE 999 TO BEST-DIST.
00139              PERFORM CHASE-EVAL THRU CHASE-EVAL-EXIT
00140                  VARYING WS-J FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-J > CAND-N.
00141          CHASE-EVAL.
00142              MOVE CAND (WS-J) TO WS-K.
00143              COMPUTE G-NX = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-K) - 1.
00144              COMPUTE G-NY = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-K) - 1.
00145              COMPUTE DIST-X = G-NX - PLAYER-X.
00146              IF DIST-X < 0
00147                  COMPUTE DIST-X = 0 - DIST-X.
00148              COMPUTE DIST-Y = G-NY - PLAYER-Y.
00149              IF DIST-Y < 0
00150                  COMPUTE DIST-Y = 0 - DIST-Y.
00151              COMPUTE DIST-T = DIST-X + DIST-Y.
00152              IF DIST-T < BEST-DIST
00153                  MOVE DIST-T TO BEST-DIST
00154                  MOVE WS-K TO WS-D.
00155          CHASE-EVAL-EXIT.
00156              EXIT.
00157          STEP-GHOST.
00158              COMPUTE GH-X (WS-G) = GH-X (WS-G) + DIR-DX (WS-D) - 1.
00159              COMPUTE GH-Y (WS-G) = GH-Y (WS-G) + DIR-DY (WS-D) - 1.
00160              MOVE WS-D TO GH-DIR (WS-G).   4

00001         * PACINPT : INPUT ADAPTER. ONE SYSIN CARD -> ONE REQUEST.
00002         * OPS : OPEN  READ  CLOS  PARM (CLASSIFY REQ-TEXT, NO I/O).
00003         * REQ-KIND: MOV (REQ-DIR U/D/L/R)  QUI  CFG (REQ-LEVEL,
00004         *   REQ-COUNT)  INV  EOF, AND IN TSO MODE RDR (BLANK LINE =
00005         *   REDRAW REQUEST). REQ-CHAR HOLDS THE COMMAND'S FIRST BYTE.
00006         * TSC (MVS-G1): READ ASKS PACPLAT RECV FOR ONE FULL-SCREEN INPUT;
00007         *   ENTER = THE TYPED FIELD, CLASSIFIED AS A CARD; ANY OTHER KEY
00008         *   (PA2 RESHOW) = RDR; AN ERROR LEAVES TSC AND READS A LINE.
00009         * ONLY THIS MODULE KNOWS ABOUT THE TERMINAL/CARD SOURCE.
00010          IDENTIFICATION DIVISION.
00011          PROGRAM-ID. PACINPT.
00012          ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
00013          CONFIGURATION SECTION.
00014          SOURCE-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00015          OBJECT-COMPUTER. IBM-370.
00016          INPUT-OUTPUT SECTION.
00017          FILE-CONTROL.
00018              SELECT COMMAND-FILE ASSIGN TO UT-S-SYSIN.
00019          DATA DIVISION.
00020          FILE SECTION.
00021          FD  COMMAND-FILE
00022              RECORDING MODE IS F
00023              LABEL RECORDS ARE OMITTED
00024              RECORD CONTAINS 80 CHARACTERS
00025              DATA RECORD IS COMMAND-RECORD.
00026          01  COMMAND-RECORD        PIC X(80).
00027          WORKING-STORAGE SECTION.
00028          01  WS-CARD.
00029              05  CMD-CHAR          PIC X.
00030              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00031              05  CMD-LEVEL         PIC X.
00032              05  FILLER            PIC X.
00033              05  CMD-COUNT         PIC X.
00034              05  FILLER            PIC X(75).
00035          01  RAW-CARD REDEFINES WS-CARD.
00036              05  RAW-CHAR          PIC X OCCURS 80 TIMES.
00037          01  NORM-REC.
00038              05  NR-CHAR           PIC X OCCURS 80 TIMES.
00039          01  WS-OFF                PIC 99.
00040          01  WS-I                  PIC 99.
00041          01  WS-J                  PIC 99.
00042          01  WS-LIM                PIC 99.
00043          LINKAGE SECTION.
00044          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00045 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00046 C        01  PAC-REQ.
00047 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00048 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00049 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00050 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00051 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00052 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00053 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00054 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).   2


00055 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00056 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00057 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00058 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00059 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00060 C            05  REQ-HILITE            PIC X.
00061          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ.
00062          MAIN-PARA.
00063              IF REQ-OP = 'OPEN'
00064                  OPEN INPUT COMMAND-FILE
00065              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ' AND REQ-MODE = 'TSC'
00066                  PERFORM TSC-READ THRU TSC-READ-EXIT
00067              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'READ'
00068                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00069              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLOS'
00070                  CLOSE COMMAND-FILE
00071              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PARM'
00072                  MOVE SPACES TO WS-CARD
00073                  MOVE REQ-TEXT TO WS-CARD
00074                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00075              GOBACK.
00076          DO-READ.
00077              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-KIND.
00078              READ COMMAND-FILE AT END MOVE 'EOF' TO REQ-KIND.
00079              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00080                  MOVE COMMAND-RECORD TO WS-CARD.
00081              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'BAT'
00082                  PERFORM NORMALIZE THRU NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00083              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'BAT'
00084                                   AND WS-CARD = SPACES
00085                  MOVE 'RDR' TO REQ-KIND.
00086              IF REQ-KIND = SPACES
00087                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00088          DO-READ-EXIT.
00089              EXIT.
00090          TSC-READ.
00091              MOVE 'RECV' TO REQ-OP.
00092              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00093              MOVE 'READ' TO REQ-OP.
00094              IF REQ-INKEY = 'X'
00095                  PERFORM DO-READ THRU DO-READ-EXIT
00096                  GO TO TSC-READ-EXIT.
00097              MOVE 'RDR' TO REQ-KIND.
00098              IF REQ-INKEY NOT = 'E'
00099                  GO TO TSC-READ-EXIT.
00100              MOVE SPACES TO WS-CARD.
00101              MOVE REQ-INTEXT TO WS-CARD.
00102              PERFORM NORMALIZE THRU NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00103              IF WS-CARD NOT = SPACES
00104                  PERFORM CLASSIFY THRU CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00105          TSC-READ-EXIT.
00106              EXIT.
00107          NORMALIZE.
00108         * TSO/TSC ONLY: TOLERATE LEADING BLANKS/NON-PRINTABLE BYTES TYPED
00109         * BEFORE THE COMMAND: START AT THE FIRST BYTE > BLANK (1-8).
00110              MOVE 1 TO WS-OFF.
00111              PERFORM FIND-START UNTIL WS-OFF > 8   3


00112                  OR RAW-CHAR (WS-OFF) > SPACE.
00113              IF WS-OFF > 1 AND WS-OFF < 9
00114                  PERFORM SHIFT-LEFT THRU SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00115          NORMALIZE-EXIT.
00116              EXIT.
00117          FIND-START.
00118              ADD 1 TO WS-OFF.
00119          SHIFT-LEFT.
00120              MOVE SPACES TO NORM-REC.
00121              COMPUTE WS-LIM = 81 - WS-OFF.
00122              PERFORM COPY-CHAR VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1
00123                  UNTIL WS-I > WS-LIM.
00124              MOVE NORM-REC TO WS-CARD.
00125          SHIFT-LEFT-EXIT.
00126              EXIT.
00127          COPY-CHAR.
00128              COMPUTE WS-J = WS-I + WS-OFF - 1.
00129              MOVE RAW-CHAR (WS-J) TO NR-CHAR (WS-I).
00130          CLASSIFY.
00131              MOVE CMD-CHAR TO REQ-CHAR.
00132              MOVE 'INV' TO REQ-KIND.
00133              IF CMD-CHAR = 'U' OR CMD-CHAR = 'u'
00134                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00135                  MOVE 'U' TO REQ-DIR
00136              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'D' OR CMD-CHAR = 'd'
00137                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00138                  MOVE 'D' TO REQ-DIR
00139              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'L' OR CMD-CHAR = 'l'
00140                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00141                  MOVE 'L' TO REQ-DIR
00142              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'R' OR CMD-CHAR = 'r'
00143                  MOVE 'MOV' TO REQ-KIND
00144                  MOVE 'R' TO REQ-DIR
00145              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'Q' OR CMD-CHAR = 'q'
00146                  MOVE 'QUI' TO REQ-KIND
00147              ELSE IF CMD-CHAR = 'A'
00148                  MOVE 'CFG' TO REQ-KIND
00149                  PERFORM SET-CONFIG THRU SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00150          CLASSIFY-EXIT.
00151              EXIT.
00152          SET-CONFIG.
00153              IF CMD-LEVEL = '0'
00154                  MOVE 0 TO REQ-LEVEL
00155              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '2'
00156                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-LEVEL
00157              ELSE IF CMD-LEVEL = '3'
00158                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-LEVEL
00159              ELSE
00160                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-LEVEL.
00161              IF CMD-COUNT = '2'
00162                  MOVE 2 TO REQ-COUNT
00163              ELSE IF CMD-COUNT = '3'
00164                  MOVE 3 TO REQ-COUNT
00165              ELSE
00166                  MOVE 1 TO REQ-COUNT.
00167          SET-CONFIG-EXIT.
00168              EXIT.   4

00001         * PACDISP : DISPLAY ADAPTER (PLAIN TEXT, LEVEL G0). READS THE

00107 C                10  GH-Y              PIC 99.
00108 C                10  GH-DIR            PIC 9.
00109          01  PAC-REQ COPY PACREQ.
00110 C       * DRIVER REQUEST BLOCK: OP CODE IN, INPUT RESULT OUT.
00111 C        01  PAC-REQ.   3


00112 C            05  REQ-OP                PIC X(4).
00113 C            05  REQ-KIND              PIC X(3).
00114 C            05  REQ-DIR               PIC X.
00115 C            05  REQ-CHAR              PIC X.
00116 C            05  REQ-LEVEL             PIC 9.
00117 C            05  REQ-COUNT             PIC 9.
00118 C            05  REQ-MODE              PIC X(3).
00119 C            05  REQ-TEXT              PIC X(8).
00120 C            05  REQ-METRIC            PIC X.
00121 C            05  REQ-COLOR             PIC X.
00122 C            05  REQ-INKEY             PIC X.
00123 C            05  REQ-INTEXT.
00124 C                10  REQ-INTEXT-CH     PIC X OCCURS 8 TIMES.
00125 C            05  REQ-HILITE            PIC X.
00126          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ.
00127          MAIN-PARA.
00128              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSC' OR REQ-MODE = 'TSD'
00129                  PERFORM SCREEN-OP THRU SCREEN-OP-EXIT
00130              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'HEAD'
00131                  DISPLAY 'PACMAN MVS-6 SMART GHOSTS'
00132                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00133                  ADD 25 TO M-CHARS
00134              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'STAT'
00135                  PERFORM SHOW-STATE THRU SHOW-STATE-EXIT
00136              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00137                  DISPLAY 'INVALID COMMAND ' REQ-CHAR
00138                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00139                  ADD 17 TO M-CHARS
00140              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'END '
00141                  PERFORM SHOW-END THRU SHOW-END-EXIT
00142              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PRMT'
00143                  PERFORM SHOW-PROMPT THRU SHOW-PROMPT-EXIT.
00144              GOBACK.
00145          SCREEN-OP.
00146              IF REQ-OP = 'STAT'
00147                  ADD 1 TO M-FRAMES
00148              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'INVL'
00149                  CALL 'PACSCR' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00150              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'PRMT'
00151                  CALL 'PACSCR' USING PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00152                  ADD DS-LEN TO M-CHARS
00153                  MOVE 'SEND' TO REQ-OP
00154                  CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ PAC-DS
00155                  MOVE 'PRMT' TO REQ-OP
00156              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'END '
00157                  MOVE 'FSOF' TO REQ-OP
00158                  CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ
00159                  MOVE 'END ' TO REQ-OP
00160                  PERFORM SHOW-END THRU SHOW-END-EXIT.
00161          SCREEN-OP-EXIT.
00162              EXIT.
00163          NEW-SCREEN.
00164              MOVE REQ-OP TO WS-SAVE-OP.
00165              MOVE 'CLS ' TO REQ-OP.
00166              ADD 1 TO M-CLEARS.
00167              CALL 'PACPLAT' USING PAC-REQ.
00168              MOVE WS-SAVE-OP TO REQ-OP.   4


00169          SHOW-PROMPT.
00170              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00171                  DISPLAY 'CMD (U/D/L/R/Q)?'
00172                  ADD 1 TO M-LINES
00173                  ADD 16 TO M-CHARS.
00174          SHOW-PROMPT-EXIT.
00175              EXIT.
00176          SHOW-END.
00177              IF F-LOST = 1
00178                  DISPLAY 'LOST'
00179                  ADD 4 TO M-CHARS
00180              ELSE IF F-WON = 1
00181                  DISPLAY 'WIN'
00182                  ADD 3 TO M-CHARS
00183              ELSE IF F-QUIT = 1
00184                  DISPLAY 'QUIT'
00185                  ADD 4 TO M-CHARS
00186              ELSE
00187                  DISPLAY 'EOF'
00188                  ADD 3 TO M-CHARS.
00189              DISPLAY 'TURNS ' TICK-COUNT.
00190              DISPLAY 'SCORE ' SCORE.
00191              DISPLAY 'LIVES ' LIVES.
00192              ADD 4 TO M-LINES.
00193              ADD 26 TO M-CHARS.
00194              IF REQ-METRIC = 'M'
00195                  PERFORM SHOW-METRICS.
00196          SHOW-END-EXIT.
00197              EXIT.
00198          SHOW-METRICS.
00199              MOVE M-FRAMES TO MT-FRAMES.
00200              MOVE M-LINES TO MT-LINES.
00201              MOVE M-CHARS TO MT-CHARS.
00202              MOVE M-CLEARS TO MT-CLEARS.
00203              DISPLAY WS-MET.
00204          SHOW-STATE.
00205              ADD 1 TO M-FRAMES.
00206              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO' AND LAST-RESULT NOT = 'START  '
00207                  PERFORM NEW-SCREEN.
00208              MOVE TICK-COUNT TO ST-TURN.
00209              MOVE LAST-CMD TO ST-CMD.
00210              MOVE LAST-RESULT TO ST-RESULT.
00211              MOVE PLAYER-X TO ST-X.
00212              MOVE PLAYER-Y TO ST-Y.
00213              MOVE SCORE TO ST-SCORE.
00214              MOVE GH-X (1) TO ST-GX.
00215              MOVE GH-Y (1) TO ST-GY.
00216              MOVE LIVES TO ST-LIVES.
00217              MOVE GAME-STATE TO ST-STATE.
00218              MOVE PELLETS-LEFT TO ST-LEFT.
00219              DISPLAY WS-STATUS-LINE.
00220              ADD 1 TO M-LINES.
00221              ADD 61 TO M-CHARS.
00222              IF GHOST-COUNT > 1
00223                  PERFORM SHOW-EXTRA THRU SHOW-EXTRA-EXIT.
00224              PERFORM SHOW-ROW VARYING WS-ROW FROM 1 BY 1
00225                  UNTIL WS-ROW > 10.   5


00226          SHOW-STATE-EXIT.
00227              EXIT.
00228          SHOW-ROW.
00229              MOVE MAP-ROW (WS-ROW) TO WS-LINE.
00230              IF WS-ROW = PLAYER-Y
00231                  MOVE 'P' TO WS-LINE-CELL (PLAYER-X).
00232              PERFORM OVERLAY-GHOST THRU OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT
00233                  VARYING WS-G FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-G > GHOST-COUNT.
00234              DISPLAY WS-LINE.
00235              ADD 1 TO M-LINES.
00236              ADD 20 TO M-CHARS.
00237          OVERLAY-GHOST.
00238              IF WS-ROW = GH-Y (WS-G)
00239                  MOVE GH-X (WS-G) TO WS-GX
00240                  MOVE 'G' TO WS-LINE-CELL (WS-GX).
00241          OVERLAY-GHOST-EXIT.
00242              EXIT.
00243          SHOW-EXTRA.
00244              MOVE GH-X (2) TO XL2-X.
00245              MOVE GH-Y (2) TO XL2-Y.
00246              ADD 1 TO M-LINES.
00247              IF GHOST-COUNT = 2
00248                  DISPLAY WS-XL2
00249                  ADD 8 TO M-CHARS
00250              ELSE
00251                  MOVE GH-X (3) TO XL3-X
00252                  MOVE GH-Y (3) TO XL3-Y
00253                  DISPLAY WS-XL2 WS-XL3
00254                  ADD 17 TO M-CHARS.
00255          SHOW-EXTRA-EXIT.
00256              EXIT.   6

00001         * PACPLAT : PLATFORM ADAPTER (MVS 3.8J). OPS : INIT FINI CLS
00002         * SEND RECV FSOF.  INIT validates REQ-MODE (BAT, TSO, TSD) and,
00003         * for TSO with REQ-COLOR A(UTO)/Y(ES), tries the TSC colour mode:
00004         * full-screen on (PACFSIO FSON); AUTO also needs QCode X'86' in
00005         * the Query Reply. Any failure leaves TSO line mode (monochrome
00006         * fallback). SEND/RECV move one TSC screen / one inbound; in TSD
00007         * (batch dry run) SEND prints the data stream in hex. An error in
00008         * TSC restores line mode and falls back to TSO. FSOF and FINI
00009         * always restore line mode if full-screen is active (PACFSIO
00010         * also restores it from an ESTAE exit on abend). CLS (TSO only)

00081          PROCEDURE DIVISION USING PAC-REQ PAC-DS.
00082          MAIN-PARA.
00083              IF REQ-OP = 'INIT'
00084                  PERFORM DO-INIT THRU DO-INIT-EXIT
00085              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'FINI'
00086                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT
00087                  MOVE 0 TO RETURN-CODE
00088              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'CLS ' AND REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00089                  CALL 'PACTSO' USING TSO-OP TSO-RC
00090              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'SEND'
00091                  PERFORM DO-SEND THRU DO-SEND-EXIT
00092              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'RECV'
00093                  PERFORM DO-RECV THRU DO-RECV-EXIT
00094              ELSE IF REQ-OP = 'FSOF'
00095                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00096              GOBACK.
00097          DO-INIT.
00098              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSO' AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSD'
00099                  MOVE 'BAT' TO REQ-MODE.
00100              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSO'
00101                      AND (REQ-COLOR = 'A' OR REQ-COLOR = 'Y')
00102                  PERFORM TRY-COLOUR THRU TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00103         * MVS-G2: HIGHLIGHT ONLY IN TSC (OR THE TSD DRY RUN) AND ONLY WHEN
00104         * ASKED: Y, OR A WITH QCODE X'87' IN THE QUERY REPLY (SET IN
00105         * TRY-COLOUR). ANYTHING ELSE IS N.
00106              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC' AND REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSD'
00107                  MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00108              IF REQ-HILITE NOT = 'Y'
00109                  MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00110          DO-INIT-EXIT.
00111              EXIT.   3


00112          TRY-COLOUR.
00113              MOVE 'FSON' TO FS-OP.
00114              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00115              IF FS-RC NOT = 0
00116                  GO TO TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00117              MOVE 1 TO FS-ACTIVE.
00118              IF REQ-COLOR = 'Y'
00119                  MOVE 'TSC' TO REQ-MODE
00120                  GO TO TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00121              MOVE 'QRY ' TO FS-OP.
00122              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00123              MOVE 0 TO HAS-COLOUR.
00124              MOVE 0 TO HAS-HILITE.
00125              IF FS-RC = 0
00126                  PERFORM FIND-COLOUR THRU FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00127              IF HAS-COLOUR = 1 AND HAS-HILITE = 1 AND REQ-HILITE = 'A'
00128                  MOVE 'Y' TO REQ-HILITE.
00129              IF HAS-COLOUR = 1
00130                  MOVE 'TSC' TO REQ-MODE
00131              ELSE
00132                  PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00133          TRY-COLOUR-EXIT.
00134              EXIT.
00135          FIND-COLOUR.
00136         * QUERY REPLY = AID X'88' (136) + STRUCTURED FIELDS: LENGTH(2),
00137         * X'81' (129), QCODE. COLOUR SUPPORT = QCODE X'86' (134).
00138              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00139              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00140              IF WS-V NOT = 136
00141                  GO TO FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00142              MOVE 2 TO WS-P.
00143              MOVE 0 TO WS-STOP.
00144              PERFORM NEXT-SF THRU NEXT-SF-EXIT UNTIL WS-STOP = 1.
00145          FIND-COLOUR-EXIT.
00146              EXIT.
00147          NEXT-SF.
00148              COMPUTE WS-LIM = WS-P + 3.
00149              IF WS-LIM > FS-LEN
00150                  MOVE 1 TO WS-STOP
00151                  GO TO NEXT-SF-EXIT.
00152              MOVE WS-P TO WS-K.
00153              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00154              COMPUTE WS-L = WS-V * 256.
00155              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00156              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00157              ADD WS-V TO WS-L.
00158              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00159              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00160              MOVE WS-V TO WS-ID.
00161              ADD 1 TO WS-K.
00162              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00163              MOVE WS-V TO WS-QC.
00164              IF WS-ID = 129 AND WS-QC = 134
00165                  MOVE 1 TO HAS-COLOUR.
00166              IF WS-ID = 129 AND WS-QC = 135
00167                  MOVE 1 TO HAS-HILITE.
00168              IF WS-L < 4   4


00169                  MOVE 1 TO WS-STOP
00170              ELSE
00171                  ADD WS-L TO WS-P.
00172          NEXT-SF-EXIT.
00173              EXIT.
00174          DO-SEND.
00175              IF REQ-MODE = 'TSD'
00176                  PERFORM PRINT-STREAM THRU PRINT-STREAM-EXIT
00177                  GO TO DO-SEND-EXIT.
00178              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC'
00179                  GO TO DO-SEND-EXIT.
00180              MOVE 'SEND' TO FS-OP.
00181              MOVE DS-LEN TO FS-LEN.
00182              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP DS-BUF FS-LEN FS-RC.
00183              IF FS-RC NOT = 0
00184                  PERFORM FALL-BACK.
00185          DO-SEND-EXIT.
00186              EXIT.
00187          DO-RECV.
00188         * REQ-INKEY: E = ENTER (REQ-INTEXT = TYPED FIELD), P = ANY OTHER
00189         * AID (PA2 RESHOW ...), X = ERROR (NOW BACK IN TSO LINE MODE).
00190              MOVE SPACES TO REQ-INTEXT.
00191              MOVE 'X' TO REQ-INKEY.
00192              IF REQ-MODE NOT = 'TSC'
00193                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00194              MOVE 'RECV' TO FS-OP.
00195              CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC.
00196              IF FS-RC NOT = 0 OR FS-LEN < 1
00197                  PERFORM FALL-BACK
00198                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00199              MOVE 'P' TO REQ-INKEY.
00200              MOVE 1 TO WS-K.
00201              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00202              IF WS-V NOT = 125
00203                  GO TO DO-RECV-EXIT.
00204              MOVE 'E' TO REQ-INKEY.
00205         * READ MODIFIED: AID, CURSOR(2), SBA X'11'(17) + ADDRESS(2), DATA.
00206              MOVE 4 TO WS-K.
00207              PERFORM BYTE-VAL.
00208              IF FS-LEN > 6 AND WS-V = 17
00209                  PERFORM COPY-TEXT THRU COPY-TEXT-EXIT
00210                      VARYING WS-I FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-I > 8.
00211          DO-RECV-EXIT.
00212              EXIT.
00213          COPY-TEXT.
00214              COMPUTE WS-K = WS-I + 6.
00215              IF WS-K NOT > FS-LEN
00216                  MOVE IN-CH (WS-K) TO REQ-INTEXT-CH (WS-I).
00217          COPY-TEXT-EXIT.
00218              EXIT.
00219          DO-FSOF.
00220              IF FS-ACTIVE = 1
00221                  MOVE 'FSOF' TO FS-OP
00222                  CALL 'PACFSIO' USING FS-OP IN-AREA FS-LEN FS-RC
00223                  MOVE 0 TO FS-ACTIVE.
00224          DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00225              EXIT.   5


00226          FALL-BACK.
00227              PERFORM DO-FSOF THRU DO-FSOF-EXIT.
00228              MOVE 'TSO' TO REQ-MODE.
00229              MOVE 'N' TO REQ-HILITE.
00230          BYTE-VAL.
00231              MOVE IN-CH (WS-K) TO HX-B.
00232              MOVE HEXN TO WS-V.
00233          PRINT-STREAM.
00234         * TSD DRY RUN: THE WHOLE DATA STREAM, 32 BYTES PER LINE IN HEX.

00236              DISPLAY LEN-LINE.

00239          PRINT-STREAM-EXIT.
00240              EXIT.
00241          PRINT-LINE.
00242              MOVE SPACES TO HEX-LINE.

00244              MOVE 0 TO WS-O.
00245              PERFORM PRINT-BYTE UNTIL WS-O > 62 OR WS-K > DS-LEN.
00246              DISPLAY HEX-LINE.
00247          PRINT-BYTE.
00248              MOVE DS-BYTE (WS-K) TO HX-B.
00249              DIVIDE HEXN BY 16 GIVING WS-Q REMAINDER WS-R.
00250              ADD 1 TO WS-Q.
00251              ADD 1 TO WS-R.
00252              ADD 1 TO WS-O.
00253              MOVE HEXDIG (WS-Q) TO HL-CH (WS-O).
00254              ADD 1 TO WS-O.
00255              MOVE HEXDIG (WS-R) TO HL-CH (WS-O).
00256              ADD 1 TO WS-K.   6

00001         * PACSCR : MVS-G1 TSC RENDERER (DISPLAY LAYER). COMPOSES THE WHOLE
00002         * 80 X 24 COLOUR SCREEN AS A 3270 DATA STREAM IN PAC-DS FROM THE
00003         * GAME STATE (READ ONLY). COLOURS AND LAYOUT COME ONLY FROM THE
00004         * COPYBOOK PACPAL. CALLED BY PACDISP:  CALL 'PACSCR' USING
00005         * PAC-STATE PAC-REQ PAC-DS.  OPS: INVL (REMEMBER THE INVALID
00006         * COMMAND FOR THE NEXT SCREEN)  PRMT (COMPOSE THE SCREEN).

00020 C       * COLOURS ARE 3270 SET-ATTRIBUTE VALUES (X'F1'-X'F7' = 241-247),
00021 C       * CONFIRMED LIVE BY PROBE E4. LAYOUT FOR AN 80 X 24 SCREEN (E5).

00033 C       * =241, X'F2' REVERSE =242, X'F4' UNDERSCORE =244; 0 = NONE).

00035 C            05  HI-GHOST              PIC 999 VALUE 242.
